k1, k2, k3 = None, None, None
n = len(nums):
k1 = nums[0]
for i in range(1, n):
    if nums[i] > k1:
        k3 = k2
        k2 = k1
        k1 = nums[i]
    elif (k2 is None) or (nums[i] > k2):
        k3 = k2
        k2 = nums[i]
    elif (k3 is None) or (nums[i] > k3):
        k3 = nums[i]


if k3 != None:
    print(k3)
else:
    print(k1)

var a = [1, 1, 2]

var d = 0
for (i = 0; i < len(a); i++) {
  if (a[i] == a[i+1]) {
    // shift to left
    for (j = i; j < len(a) - i - 1; j++) {
      a[j] = a[j+1]
    }
    a[j+1] = _
    d += 1
  }
}

return len(a) - d ^hvhXBURj

def remove_duplicates(arr):
    if not arr:
        return arr

    write_index = 1  # РєСѓРґР° Р·Р°РїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚

    for read_index in range(1, len(arr)):
        if arr[read_index] != arr[read_index - 1]:
            arr[write_index] = arr[read_index]
            write_index += 1

    # "РѕС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј" С…РІРѕСЃС‚
    for i in range(write_index, len(arr)):
        arr[i] = None  # РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ 0, "" вЂ” С‡С‚Рѕ С‚РµР±Рµ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ

    return arr ^CPHWyt7z

in: int[] arr
int i, j

while(true) {
    while (j < #arr && arr[i] == arr[j]) j++
    if (j == #arr - 1) break
    i++
    arr[i] = arr[j]
}

return arr

in int[] arr

int soltair(left, right) {
    j = right - 1
    pindex = left
    for (int i = left; i < j; i++) {
        if(arr[i] > arr[pindex]) {
            while(arr[j] > arr[pindex]) j--
            if (j == pindex) pindex = i
            else if (i == pindex) pindex = j
            swap(arr[i], arr[j])
            j--
        }
    }
    return pindex
}


l = 0, r = #
foreva {
    pindex = soltair(l, r)
    if pindex == 3 return arr[3]
    if (pindex < 3) l = pindex + 1 
    if (pindex > 3) r = pindex
}

------------------------------------------------------------------------
------------------------------------------------------------------------


int soltair(int left, int right) {
    int pivot = arr[right];
    int pindex = left;

    for (int i = left; i < right; i++) {
        if (arr[i] <= pivot) {
            swap(arr[i], arr[pindex]);
            pindex++;
        }
    }

    swap(arr[pindex], arr[right]);
    return pindex;
}
